30-00-00-040-801MMEL 30-01 (DDPG) Preparation - Wing Anti-Ice Valves Inoperative ATA 30 · Task 040-801

Task Description

2. MMEL 30-01 (DDPG) Preparation - Wing Anti-Ice Valves Inoperative (Figure 901)

A. General

(1) This task gives the maintenance steps which prepare the airplane for flight with the wing anti-ice valves inoperative. (2) The location of the wing anti-ice valve relative to the engine exhaust area prohibits personnel from accessing the valve when the associated engine is running.

B. References

Reference Title 27-81-00-080-801 Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Removal (P/B 201) 27-81-00-480-801 Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Installation (P/B 201) 71-00-00-800-807-F00 Start the Engine Procedure (Selection) (P/B 201) 71-00-00-800-810-F00 Start the Engine Procedure (Engine Cross Bleed Start) (P/B 201) 30-00-00

C. Location Zones

Zone Area 522 Left Wing - Slat No. 4 622 Right Wing - Slat No. 5

D. Access Panels

Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door

E. Prepare to Dispatch With a Wing Anti-Ice Valve Closed

SUBTASK 30-00-00-840-001 (1) Prepare to close the wing anti-ice valve: WARNING MAKE SURE THAT YOU DO THE PROCEDURE TO DEACTIVATE THE LEADING EDGE SLATS. IF THE LEADING EDGE SLATS MOVE, INJURY TO PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR. (a) Do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Installation, TASK 27-81-00-480-801. (b) Open this circuit breaker and install safety tag: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (c) For the left wing, open this access panel: Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door (d) For the right wing, open this access panel: Number Name/Location 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door SUBTASK 30-00-00-020-001 (2) Disconnect the electrical connector from the wing anti-ice valve and stow the connector. NOTE: The associated wing anti-ice VALVE OPEN light will illuminate bright. SUBTASK 30-00-00-040-001 (3) Move the red lever on the wing anti-ice valve to the CLOSED position. SUBTASK 30-00-00-840-002 (4) Return the airplane to its usual condition with a wing anti-ice valve locked closed: (a) For the left wing, close this access panel: Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door (b) For the right wing, close this access panel: Number Name/Location 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door 30-00-00 (c) Remove the safety tag and close this circuit breaker: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (d) Activate the LE slats. To activate them, do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Removal, TASK 27-81-00-080-801. SUBTASK 30-00-00-930-001 (5) Put an INOP OFF placard on the WING ANTI-ICE switch. F. Prepare to Dispatch With the Left Wing Anti-Ice Valve Locked Open SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-001 (1) To open the valve when APU bleed air will be used to start the engine, do these steps: WARNING MAKE SURE THAT YOU DO THE PROCEDURE TO DEACTIVATE THE LEADING EDGE SLATS. IF THE LEADING EDGE SLATS MOVE, INJURY TO PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR. (a) Do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Installation, TASK 27-81-00-480-801. (b) Open this circuit breaker and install safety tag: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (c) Open this access panel: Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door (d) Disconnect the electrical connector from the left wing anti-ice valve and stow the connector. NOTE: The associated wing anti-ice VALVE OPEN light will illuminate bright. (e) Move the red lever on the left wing anti-ice valve to the CLOSED position. WARNING MAKE SURE THAT ALL PERSONNEL AND EQUIPMENT ARE CLEAR OF THE RIGHT ENGINE DANGER AREA. THE ENGINE INTAKE AND EXHAUST CAN CAUSE INJURY PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE EQUIPMENT IN THE DANGER AREAS. (f) Make sure that all personnel are cleared from the right engine danger area. (g) For the right engine, do this task: Start the Engine Procedure (Selection), TASK 71-00-00-800-807-F00. (h) Close the isolation and APU bleed air valves to depressurize the left pneumatic duct. (i) After the pneumatic duct is depressurized, move the wing anti-ice valve to the open position. (j) Close this access panel: Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door 30-00-00 (k) Remove the safety tag and close this circuit breaker: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (l) Activate the LE slats. To activate them, do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Removal, TASK 27-81-00-080-801. WARNING MAKE SURE THAT ALL PERSONNEL AND EQUIPMENT ARE CLEAR OF THE LEFT ENGINE DANGER AREA. THE ENGINE INTAKE AND EXHAUST CAN CAUSE INJURY PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE EQUIPMENT IN THE DANGER AREAS. (m) Make sure that all personnel are cleared from the left engine danger area. (n) T ell the flight deck that the left engine can be started with this task: Start the Engine Procedure (Engine Cross Bleed Start), TASK 71-00-00-800-810-F00. SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-002 (2) To open the valve when ground service air will be used to start the engine, do these steps: WARNING MAKE SURE THAT YOU DO THE PROCEDURE TO DEACTIVATE THE LEADING EDGE SLATS. IF THE LEADING EDGE SLATS MOVE, INJURY TO PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR. (a) Do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Installation, TASK 27-81-00-480-801. (b) Open this circuit breaker and install safety tag: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (c) Open this access panel: Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door (d) Disconnect the electrical connector from the left wing anti-ice valve and stow the connector. NOTE: The associated wing anti-ice VALVE OPEN light will illuminate bright. (e) Move the red lever on the left wing anti-ice valve to the OPEN position. (f) Close this access panel: Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door (g) Remove the safety tag and close this circuit breaker: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (h) Activate the LE slats. To activate them, do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Removal, TASK 27-81-00-080-801. 30-00-00 WARNING MAKE SURE THAT ALL PERSONNEL AND EQUIPMENT ARE CLEAR OF THE ENGINE DANGER AREAS. THE ENGINE INTAKE AND EXHAUST CAN CAUSE INJURIES TO PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT IN THE DANGER AREAS. (i) Make sure that all personnel are cleared from the engines danger areas. (j) T ell the flight deck that the right engine can be started (isolation valve closed) and then the left engine can be started with this task: Start the Engine Procedure (Engine Cross Bleed Start), TASK 71-00-00-800-810-F00. SUBTASK 30-00-00-930-002 (3) Put an INOP ON placard on the WING ANTI-ICE switch.

G. Prepare to Dispatch With the Right Wing Anti-Ice Valve Locked Open

SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-003 (1) To open the valve when APU bleed air will be used to start the engine, do these steps: WARNING MAKE SURE THAT YOU DO THE PROCEDURE TO DEACTIVATE THE LEADING EDGE SLATS. IF THE LEADING EDGE SLATS MOVE, INJURY TO PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR. (a) Do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Installation, TASK 27-81-00-480-801. (b) Open this circuit breaker and install safety tag: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (c) Open this access panel: Number Name/Location 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door (d) Disconnect the electrical connector from the right wing anti-ice valve and stow the connector. NOTE: The associated wing anti-ice VALVE OPEN light will illuminate bright. (e) Move the red lever on the right wing anti-ice valve to the OPEN position. (f) Close this access panel: Number Name/Location 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door (g) Remove the safety tag and close this circuit breaker: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (h) Activate the LE slats. To activate them, do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Removal, TASK 27-81-00-080-801. 30-00-00 WARNING MAKE SURE THAT ALL PERSONNEL AND EQUIPMENT ARE CLEAR OF THE ENGINE DANGER AREAS. THE ENGINE INTAKE AND EXHAUST CAN CAUSE INJURIES TO PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT IN THE DANGER AREAS. (i) Make sure that all personnel are cleared from the engines danger areas. (j) T ell the flight deck that the left engine can be started (isolation valve closed) and then the right engine can be started with this task: Start the Engine Procedure (Engine Cross Bleed Start), TASK 71-00-00-800-810-F00. SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-004 (2) To open the valve when ground service air will be used to start the engine, do these steps: WARNING MAKE SURE THAT YOU DO THE PROCEDURE TO DEACTIVATE THE LEADING EDGE SLATS. IF THE LEADING EDGE SLATS MOVE, INJURY TO PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR. (a) Do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Installation, TASK 27-81-00-480-801. (b) Open this circuit breaker and install safety tag: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (c) Open this access panel: Number Name/Location 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door (d) Disconnect the electrical connector from the right wing anti-ice valve and stow the connector. NOTE: The associated wing anti-ice VALVE OPEN light will illuminate bright. (e) Move the red lever on the right wing anti-ice valve to the CLOSED position. WARNING MAKE SURE THAT ALL PERSONNEL AND EQUIPMENT ARE CLEAR OF THE LEFT ENGINE DANGER AREA. THE ENGINE INTAKE AND EXHAUST CAN CAUSE INJURY PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE EQUIPMENT IN THE DANGER AREAS. (f) Make sure that all personnel are cleared from the left engine danger area. (g) For the left engine, do this task: Start the Engine Procedure (Selection), TASK 71-00-00-800-807-F00. (h) Remove the ground air source from the airplane. (i) Close the isolation and APU bleed air valves to depressurize the right pneumatic duct. (j) After the pneumatic duct is depressurized, manually move the wing anti-ice valve to the open position. (k) Close this access panel: Number Name/Location 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door 30-00-00 (l) Remove the safety tag and close this circuit breaker: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (m) Activate the LE slats. To activate them, do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Removal, TASK 27-81-00-080-801. WARNING MAKE SURE THAT ALL PERSONNEL AND EQUIPMENT ARE CLEAR OF THE RIGHT ENGINE DANGER AREA. THE ENGINE INTAKE AND EXHAUST CAN CAUSE INJURY PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE EQUIPMENT IN THE DANGER AREAS. (n) Make sure that all personnel are cleared from the right engine danger area. (o) T ell the flight deck that the right engine can be started with this task: Start the Engine Procedure (Engine Cross Bleed Start), TASK 71-00-00-800-810-F00. SUBTASK 30-00-00-930-003 (3) Put an INOP ON placard on the WING ANTI-ICE switch.

30-00-00-440-801MMEL 30-01 (DDPG) Restoration - Wing Anti-Ice Valves Inoperative ATA 30 · Task 440-801

Task Description

3. MMEL 30-01 (DDPG) Restoration - Wing Anti-Ice Valves Inoperative

A. General

(1) This task puts the airplane back to its usual condition after operation with the wing anti-ice valves inoperative.

B. References

Reference Title 27-81-00-080-801 Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Removal (P/B 201) 27-81-00-480-801 Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Installation (P/B 201)

C. Location Zones

Zone Area 522 Left Wing - Slat No. 4 622 Right Wing - Slat No. 5

D. Access Panels

Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door

E. Reactivate the Wing Anti-Ice Valve

SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-005 (1) Open this circuit breaker and install safety tag: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-006 (2) Set the WING ANTI-ICE switch on the anti-ice panel on the P5 overhead panel in the flight compartment to OFF . SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-007 WARNING MAKE SURE YOU DO THE PROCEDURE TO DEACTIVATE THE LE SLATS. THE LEADING EDGE SLATS CAN MOVE AND CAUSE INJURIES TO PERSONS IF THEY ARE NOT DEACTIVATED. (3) Do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Installation, TASK 27-81-00-480-801. SUBTASK 30-00-00-010-001 (4) For the left wing, open this access panel: Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door SUBTASK 30-00-00-010-002 (5) For the right wing, open this access panel: Number Name/Location 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door SUBTASK 30-00-00-420-001 (6) Connect the electrical connector to the wing anti-ice valve. 30-00-00 SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-008 (7) Remove the safety tag and close this circuit breaker: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE F. Wing Anti-Ice Valve Repair SUBTASK 30-00-00-810-001 (1) Correct the fault. (a) Find the fault code or description of the fault that occured. (b) If you find a fault code, then do these steps: 1) Go to the Fault Code Index in the applicable chapter of the FIM and find the fault code. NOTE: The first two digits of the fault code is the FIM chapter. 2) Find the task number on the same line as the fault code. 3) Go to the task in the FIM and do the steps in the task. (c) If you find a description of the fault, then do these steps: 1) Go to the Observed Fault List at the beginning of the FIM and find the best description for the fault. 2) Find the task number on the same line as the fault description. 3) Go to the task in the FIM and do the steps in the task.

G. Put the Airplane Back to Its Usual Condition

SUBTASK 30-00-00-410-001 (1) For the left wing, close this access panel: Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door SUBTASK 30-00-00-410-002 (2) For the right wing, close this access panel: Number Name/Location 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door SUBTASK 30-00-00-440-001 (3) Activate the LE slats. To activate them, do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Removal, TASK 27-81-00-080-801. SUBTASK 30-00-00-020-002 (4) If the fault was repaired, remove the INOP placard from the WING ANTI-ICE switch.

30-00-00-040-802MMEL 30-02 (DDPG) Preparation - Wing Anti-Ice Valve Position Lights Inoperative ATA 30 · Task 040-802

Task Description

4. MMEL 30-02 (DDPG) Preparation - Wing Anti-Ice Valve Position Lights Inoperative

A. General

(1) This task gives the maintenance steps which prepare the airplane for flight with the wing anti-ice valve position lights inoperative. (2) This procedure tests the valve connected to the failed position light. If both position lights are failed, both valves must be tested.

B. References

Reference Title 27-81-00-080-801 Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Removal (P/B 201) 27-81-00-480-801 Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Installation (P/B 201)

C. Location Zones

Zone Area 522 Left Wing - Slat No. 4 622 Right Wing - Slat No. 5

D. Access Panels

Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door

E. Prepare for the Procedure

SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-009 WARNING MAKE SURE YOU DO THE PROCEDURE TO DEACTIVATE THE LE SLATS. THE LEADING EDGE SLATS CAN MOVE AND CAUSE INJURIES TO PERSONS IF THEY ARE NOT DEACTIVATED. (1) Do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Installation, TASK 27-81-00-480-801. SUBTASK 30-00-00-010-003 (2) For the left wing, open this access panel: Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door SUBTASK 30-00-00-010-004 (3) For the right wing, open this access panel: Number Name/Location 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door F. Test the Wing Anti-Ice Valve SUBTASK 30-00-00-710-001 (1) Put the WING ANTI-ICE switch in the ON position. SUBTASK 30-00-00-710-002 (2) Observe the position indicator on the wing anti-ice valve and make sure the valve indicates OPEN. SUBTASK 30-00-00-710-003 (3) Put the WING ANTI-ICE switch in the OFF position. 30-00-00 SUBTASK 30-00-00-710-004 (4) Observe the position indicator on the wing anti-ice valve and make sure the valve indicates CLOSED. SUBTASK 30-00-00-930-004 (5) Attach an INOP placard to the applicable VALVE OPEN light.

G. Return the Airplane to Its Usual Condition

SUBTASK 30-00-00-010-005 (1) For the left wing, close this access panel: Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door SUBTASK 30-00-00-010-006 (2) For the right wing, close this access panel: Number Name/Location 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door SUBTASK 30-00-00-440-002 (3) Do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Removal, TASK 27-81-00-080-801.

30-00-00-440-802MMEL 30-02 (DDPG) Restoration - Wing Anti-Ice Valve Position Lights Inoperative ATA 30 · Task 440-802

Task Description

5. MMEL 30-02 (DDPG) Restoration - Wing Anti-Ice Valve Position Lights Inoperative

A. General

(1) This task puts the airplane back to its usual condition after operation with the wing anti-ice valve position lights inoperative.

B. Location Zones

Zone Area 522 Left Wing - Slat No. 4 622 Right Wing - Slat No. 5

C. Procedure

SUBTASK 30-00-00-810-002 (1) Correct the fault. (a) Find the fault code or description of the fault that occured. (b) If you find a fault code, then do these steps: 1) Go to the Fault Code Index in the applicable chapter of the FIM and find the fault code. NOTE: The first two digits of the fault code is the FIM chapter. 2) Find the task number on the same line as the fault code. 3) Go to the task in the FIM and do the steps in the task. (c) If you find a description of the fault, then do these steps: 1) Go to the Observed Fault List at the beginning of the FIM and find the best description for the fault. 2) Find the task number on the same line as the fault description. 3) Go to the task in the FIM and do the steps in the task. 30-00-00 SUBTASK 30-00-00-020-003 (2) If the fault was repaired, remove the INOP placard from the valve open light.

30-00-00-040-803MMEL 30-03 (DDPG) Preparation - Engine and Nose Cowl Anti-Ice Valves Inoperative ATA 30 · Task 040-803

Task Description

6. MMEL 30-03 (DDPG) Preparation - Engine and Nose Cowl Anti-Ice Valves Inoperative (Figure 902, Figure 903)

A. General

(1) This task gives the maintenance steps which prepare the airplane for flight with the engine anti-ice valves inoperative. (2) The engine anti-ice valves can be deactivated by locking them closed or open.

B. References

Reference Title 71-11-02-010-801-F00 Open the Fan Cowl Panels (P/B 201) 71-11-02-410-801-F00 Close the Fan Cowl Panels (P/B 201) 78-31-00-010-801-F00 Open the Thrust Reverser (Selection) (P/B 201) 78-31-00-010-804-F00 Close the Thrust Reverser (Selection) (P/B 201)

C. Consumable Materials

D. Location Zones

Zone Area 411 Engine 1 - Engine 421 Engine 2 - Engine

E. Prepare for the Flight with Engine and Nose Cowl Anti-Ice Valves Inoperative

SUBTASK 30-00-00-010-007 (1) Do this task: Open the Fan Cowl Panels, TASK 71-11-02-010-801-F00. SUBTASK 30-00-00-010-008 (2) Do this task: Open the Thrust Reverser (Selection), TASK 78-31-00-010-801-F00. SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-019 (3) Open these circuit breakers and install safety tags: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 6 C00148 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 1 & WING CONT A 7 C01001 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 1 COWL AI VALVE 30-00-00 (Continued) CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name B 6 C00149 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENGINE 2 CONTROL B 7 C01002 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 2 COWL AI VALVE SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-032 (4) Set each engine start lever in the CUTOFF position. F. Put the Engine Anti-Ice Valve Inoperative Closed SUBTASK 30-00-00-210-001 (1) To manually position the valve in the CLOSED position, do these steps: (a) Loosen the retaining screw on the manual locking assembly. (b) Move the hex shaft to the CLOSED desired position. (c) Slide the locking assembly up the shaft into the recess in the cover plate. (d) Tighten the retaining screw to 20 ±1 in-lb (2.26 ±0.11 N·m). NOTE: Boeing recommends a 1/4 inch, 6 point, closed end spanner or socket to do this step.

G. Put the Engine Anti-Ice Valve Inoperative Open

NOTE: Dispatching with an engine and nose cowl anti-ice valve inoperative open will cause the associated COWL VALVE OPEN light to illuminate bright blue, on the P5-11 panel, and associated Thermal Anti-Ice (TAI) indication to illuminate amber on the flight deck Common Display System (CDS). SUBTASK 30-00-00-210-002 (1) To manually position the valve in the OPEN position, do these steps: (a) Loosen the retaining screw on the manual locking assembly. (b) Move the hex shaft to the OPEN position. (c) Slide the locking assembly up the shaft into the recess in the cover plate. (d) Apply a small quantity of Liqui-Moly NV Thread Compound, D50179, to the threads of the retaining screw. (e) Tighten the retaining screw to 20 ±1 in-lb (2.26 ±0.11 N·m). NOTE: Boeing recommends a 1/4 inch, 6 point, closed end spanner or socket to do this step. SUBTASK 30-00-00-020-006 (2) Disconnect, cap, and stow the DP1302 ELECTRICAL CONNECTOR [3] on the ANTI-ICE PRESSURE SWITCH [4] (Figure 902). SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-010 (3) Close the HIGH STAGE VALVE [5] from the 9th stage bleed air source (Figure 903). NOTE: Only one high stage valve may be locked in the closed position for dispatch. Dispatch with the opposite high stage valve inoperative under Master Minimum Equipment List (MMEL) item 36-9 is not allowed. (a) On the MANUAL OVERRIDE/POSITION INDICATOR [6], turn the manual override nut to align the position indicator with the CLOSED position. (b) Loosen the bolt. (c) Push in the lock knob to lock the high stage valve closed. 30-00-00 (d) Apply Loctite LB 8150 anti-seize compound, D50188, or Loctite C5-A compound, D00667, or compound, D00010, or Pure Nickel Special compound, D00006, to the threads of the bolt. (e) Tighten the bolt to 50 in-lb (5.65 N·m) - 55 in-lb (6.21 N·m) to hold the lock knob in its position.

H. Secure the Engine Access Panels

SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-020 (1) Remove the safety tags and close these circuit breakers: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 6 C00148 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 1 & WING CONT A 7 C01001 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 1 COWL AI VALVE B 6 C00149 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENGINE 2 CONTROL B 7 C01002 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 2 COWL AI VALVE SUBTASK 30-00-00-010-010 (2) Do this task: Close the Thrust Reverser (Selection), TASK 78-31-00-010-804-F00. SUBTASK 30-00-00-010-009 (3) Do this task: Close the Fan Cowl Panels, TASK 71-11-02-410-801-F00.

I. Put INOP Placards on the Affected Switch and Light

SUBTASK 30-00-00-930-005 (1) Put an INOP LOCKED OPEN or INOP LOCKED CLOSED placard on the affected ENG ANTI-ICE switch.

30-00-00-440-803MMEL 30-03 (DDPG) Restoration - Engine and Nose Cowl Anti-Ice Valves Inoperative ATA 30 · Task 440-803

Task Description

7. MMEL 30-03 (DDPG) Restoration - Engine and Nose Cowl Anti-Ice Valves Inoperative (Figure 903)

A. General

(1) This task puts the airplane back to its usual condition after operation with the engine anti-ice systems inoperative.

B. References

Reference Title 71-11-02-010-801-F00 Open the Fan Cowl Panels (P/B 201) 71-11-02-410-801-F00 Close the Fan Cowl Panels (P/B 201) 78-31-00-010-801-F00 Open the Thrust Reverser (Selection) (P/B 201) 78-31-00-010-804-F00 Close the Thrust Reverser (Selection) (P/B 201)

C. Consumable Materials

D. Location Zones

Zone Area 411 Engine 1 - Engine 421 Engine 2 - Engine

E. Prepare for the Engine and Nose Cowl Anti-Ice Valves Unlocking

SUBTASK 30-00-00-010-011 (1) Do this task: Open the Fan Cowl Panels, TASK 71-11-02-010-801-F00. SUBTASK 30-00-00-010-012 (2) Do this task: Open the Thrust Reverser (Selection), TASK 78-31-00-010-801-F00. SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-021 (3) Open these circuit breakers and install safety tags: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 6 C00148 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 1 & WING CONT A 7 C01001 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 1 COWL AI VALVE B 6 C00149 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENGINE 2 CONTROL B 7 C01002 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 2 COWL AI VALVE F. Engine and Nose Cowl Anti-Ice Valves Unlocking SUBTASK 30-00-00-210-004 (1) Unlock the engine cowl anti-ice valve, do these steps: 30-00-00 (a) Loosen the retaining screw on the manual locking assembly. (b) Slide the locking assembly out of the cover plate recess. (c) Apply a small quantity of Liqui-Moly NV Thread Compound, D50179, to the threads of the retaining screw. (d) Tighten the retaining screw to 20 ±1 in-lb (2.26 ±0.11 N·m). SUBTASK 30-00-00-420-003 (2) Connect the electrical connector to the pressure switch. SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-031 (3) If it is necessary, unlock the HIGH STAGE VALVE [5] from the 9th stage bleed air source (Figure 903). (a) Loosen the bolt. (b) Pull the lock knob out to unlock the high stage valve. (c) Apply Loctite LB 8150 anti-seize compound, D50188, or Loctite C5-A compound, D00667, or compound, D00010, or Pure Nickel Special compound, D00006, to the threads of the bolt. (d) Tighten the bolt to 50 in-lb (5.65 N·m) - 55 in-lb (6.21 N·m) to hold the lock knob in its position. SUBTASK 30-00-00-860-022 (4) Remove the safety tags and close these circuit breakers: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 6 C00148 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 1 & WING CONT A 7 C01001 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 1 COWL AI VALVE B 6 C00149 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENGINE 2 CONTROL B 7 C01002 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 2 COWL AI VALVE

H. Engine Cowl Anti-Ice Valve Repair

SUBTASK 30-00-00-810-003 (1) Correct the fault. (a) Find the fault code or description of the fault that occurred. (e) Tighten the retaining screw to 20 ±1 in-lb (2.26 ±0.11 N·m). NOTE: Boeing recommends a 1/4 inch, 6 point, closed end spanner or socket to do this step. Dispatch with the opposite high stage valve inoperative under MMEL item 36-9 is not allowed. (a) On the MANUAL OVERRIDE/POSITION INDICATOR [6], turn the manual override nut to align the position indicator with the CLOSED position. (b) Loosen the bolt. (c) Push in the lock knob to lock the high stage valve closed. (d) Apply Loctite LB 8150 anti-seize compound, D50188, or Loctite C5-A compound, D00667, or compound, D00010, or Pure Nickel Special compound, D00006 to the thread of the bolt. (e) Tighten the bolt to 50 in-lb (5.65 N·m) - 55 in-lb (6.21 N·m) to hold the lock knob in its position. F. (a) Make sure that the left COWL VALVE OPEN light stays off. SUBTASK 30-11-00-020-004 (7) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 1 switch in the OFF position. SUBTASK 30-11-00-420-003 (8) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 2 switch in the ON position. (a) Make sure that the right COWL VALVE OPEN light stays off. SUBTASK 30-11-00-020-005 (9) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 2 switch in the OFF position. SUBTASK 30-11-00-040-001 (10) Install DO-NOT-OPERATE tags on the WING ANTI-ICE, ENG ANTI-ICE 1 and ENG ANTI-ICE 2 switches on the engine and wing anti-ice control panel (P5-11).

SUBTASK 30-11-00-860-006 CAUTION DO NOT KEEP THE WING TAI VALVE OPEN FOR MORE THAN 30 SECONDS WHEN THE PNEUMATIC SYSTEM IS PRESSURIZED. IF IT IS OPEN FOR MORE THAN 30 SECONDS, THE HOT AIR CAN CAUSE DAMAGE TO THE WING LEADING EDGE. (3) Do this test to make sure that the engine and wing anti-ice panel commands the anti-ice valves open: (a) Put the WING ANTI-ICE switch in the ON position. (b) Make sure that the VALVE OPEN lights come on brightly for 1 to 6 seconds. (c) Make sure that the VALVE OPEN lights become dim after 6 seconds. (d) Make sure that the two wing anti-ice valves are open. 30-11-00 SUBTASK 30-11-00-720-001 (4) Do this test to make sure that the wing anti-ice valves close when the thrust lever is advanced: (a) Put the left thrust lever in the fully advanced position. 1) Make sure that the VALVE OPEN lights become bright. (b) Put the left thrust lever in the idle position. 1) Make sure that the VALVE OPEN lights become dim after 6 seconds. (c) Put the right thrust lever in the fully advanced position. 1) Make sure that the VALVE OPEN lights become bright. (d) Put the right thrust lever in the idle position. 1) Make sure that the VALVE OPEN lights become dim after 6 seconds. SUBTASK 30-11-00-720-002 (5) Do this test to make sure that the wing anti-ice valves close when the left duct becomes too hot. (a) Open this circuit breaker and install safety tag: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (b) Disconnect the electrical connector, D738 from the left wing ground thermal anti-ice switch. (c) Connect a Jumper Wire, STD-6286 (or equivalent electrical jumper, STD-4931), between pins 1 and 2 of the electrical connector, D738. (d) Remove the safety tag and close this circuit breaker: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (e) Make sure that the L and R VALVE OPEN lights come on brightly. (f) Open this circuit breaker and install safety tag: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (g) Remove the Jumper Wire, STD-6286, from the electrical connector, D738. (h) Connect the electrical connector, D738 to the left wing ground thermal anti-ice switch. (i) Remove the safety tag and close this circuit breaker: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (j) Make sure that the L and R VALVE OPEN lights are dim. SUBTASK 30-11-00-720-003 (6) Do this test to make sure that the wing anti-ice valves close when the right duct becomes too hot. 30-11-00 (a) Open this circuit breaker and install safety tag: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (b) Disconnect the electrical connector, D736 from the right wing ground thermal anti-ice switch. (c) Connect a Jumper Wire, STD-6286, between pins 1 and 2 of the electrical connector, D736. (d) Remove the safety tag and close this circuit breaker: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (e) Make sure that the L and R VALVE OPEN lights come on brightly. (h) Connect the electrical connector, D736 to the right wing ground thermal anti-ice switch. (i) Remove the safety tag and close this circuit breaker: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE (j) Make sure that the L and R VALVE OPEN lights are dim. (b) Do this task: Put the Airplane in the Air Mode, TASK 32-09-00-860-801. 1) Make sure that the WING ANTI-ICE switch goes to the OFF position. 2) Make sure that the VALVE OPEN lights are bright for 1 to 6 seconds and then go off. (c) Put the WING ANTI-ICE switch in the ON position. 1) Make sure that the VALVE OPEN lights are bright for 1 to 6 seconds and then go dim. (d) Return the airplane to the ground mode, do this task: Return the Airplane to the Ground Mode, TASK 32-09-00-860-802. 30-11-00 SUBTASK 30-11-00-860-007 (8) Do these steps to make sure that the engine and wing anti-ice panel commands the anti-ice valves closed: (a) Put the WING ANTI-ICE switch in the OFF position. (b) Make sure that the VALVE OPEN lights come on brightly for 1 to 6 seconds. (c) Make sure that the VALVE OPEN lights go off after 6 seconds.

30-11-00-790-801Wing Anti-Icing Duct - Leak Test ATA 30 · Task 790-801

Task Description

3. Wing Anti-Icing Duct - Leak Test (Figure 501)

A. General

(1) This test checks for leaks in the wing anti-icing ducts in the fixed leading edge of the wings, and in the telescoping ducts. The check includes a visual inspection of the ducts and connectors. The check also includes feeling for leaks at the clamp connectors and duct sleeves.

B. References

Reference Title 36-00-00-860-802 Supply Pressure to the Pneumatic System with an External Ground Air Source (P/B 201) 36-00-00-860-803 Supply Pressure to the Pneumatic System with the APU (P/B 201) 36-00-00-860-806 Remove Pressure from the Pneumatic System (P/B 201)

C. Location Zones

Zone Area 510 Subzone - Left Wing: Leading Edge, Fwd of Front Spar, Inbd of Strut and Nacelle Gap Cover Area 520 Subzone - Left Wing: Leading Edge, Fwd of Front Spar, Outboard of Strut and Nacelle Gap Cover Area 610 Subzone - Right Wing: Leading Edge, Forward of Front Spar, Inboard of Nacelle Strut, Including Gap Cover Area 620 Subzone - Right Wing: Leading Edge, Forward of Front Spar, Outboard of Nacelle Strut

D. Access Panels

Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door

E. Prepare for the Procedure

SUBTASK 30-11-00-860-010 (1) Make sure that the thrust levers are in the IDLE position. SUBTASK 30-11-00-010-002 (2) Open these access panels: Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door F. Procedure SUBTASK 30-11-00-210-002 (1) Do a visual check of the wing thermal anti-icing duct and fittings. (a) Inspect for holes, cracks, and loose components. SUBTASK 30-11-00-840-001 (2) Supply pneumatic pressure for the ducts. 30-11-00 (a) To use the APU to pressurize the TAI duct, do this task: Supply Pressure to the Pneumatic System with the APU, TASK 36-00-00-860-803. (b) To use an external air supply to pressurize the TAI duct, do this task: Supply Pressure to the Pneumatic System with an External Ground Air Source, TASK 36-00-00-860-802. (c) Put the BLEED 1 and 2 switches on the air conditioning panel to the OFF position. (d) Put the L PACK and R PACK switches on the air conditioning panel to the OFF position. (e) Make sure the ISOLATION VALVE switch is in the OPEN or the AUTO position. SUBTASK 30-11-00-860-011 CAUTION DO NOT KEEP THE WING TAI VALVE OPEN FOR MORE THAN 30 SECONDS WHEN THE PNEUMATIC SYSTEM IS PRESSURIZED. IF IT IS OPEN FOR MORE THAN 30 SECONDS, THE HOT AIR CAN CAUSE DAMAGE TO THE WING LEADING EDGE. (3) Put the WING ANTI-ICE switch to the ON position. SUBTASK 30-11-00-200-001 (4) Do this check for leaks from the TAI duct: WARNING DO NOT TOUCH THE TAI DUCT . THE DUCT CAN GET HOT AND INJURIES TO PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR. (a) Move your hand near the TAI duct to feel for leaks. 1) Some leakage along duct seals and joints is satisfactory. 2) Leaks which can be felt 12 in. (305 mm) or more away are not satisfactory. SUBTASK 30-11-00-200-002 WARNING DO NOT TOUCH THE TAI DUCT . THE DUCT CAN GET HOT AND INJURIES TO PERSONNEL AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR. (5) Do this check for leaks around the connection between the thermal switch and the duct boss: (a) Move your hand near the thermal switch and the duct boss to feel for leaks. 1) Some leakage along duct seals and joints is satisfactory. 2) Leaks which can be felt 12 in. (305 mm) or more away are not satisfactory. SUBTASK 30-11-00-720-006 (6) Put the WING ANTI-ICE switch to the OFF position.

G. Put the Airplane Back to Its Usual Condition

SUBTASK 30-11-00-860-012 (1) Do this task to remove pneumatic pressure from the ducts: Remove Pressure from the Pneumatic System, TASK 36-00-00-860-806. SUBTASK 30-11-00-410-003 (2) Close these access panels: Number Name/Location 521AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door 30-11-00 (Continued) Number Name/Location 621AB Outboard Leading Edge Blowout Door

(d) Make sure that the solenoid valve [7] makes a clicking sound. NOTE: If the solenoid valve makes a clicking sound, then the valve is closed and operates correctly.

SUBTASK 30-11-12-410-004 WARNING OBEY THE INSTRUCTIONS IN THE PROCEDURE TO CLOSE THE THRUST REVERSERS. IF YOU DO NOT OBEY THE INSTRUCTIONS WHEN YOU CLOSE THE THRUST REVERSERS, INJURIES TO PERSONS OR DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR. (1) Do this task: Close the Thrust Reverser (Selection), TASK 78-31-00-010-804-F00.

30-11-12-710-801Ground Wing Thermal Anti-Icing (TAI) Solenoid Valve Test ATA 30 · Task 710-801

Task Description

2. Ground Wing Thermal Anti-Icing (TAI) Solenoid Valve Test (Figure 501) NOTE: This procedure is a scheduled maintenance task.

A. General

(1) This test checks for the proper operation of the ground wing TAI solenoid valve [1] .

B. References

Reference Title 32-09-00-840-801 Prepare to Put the Airplane in the Air Mode (P/B 201) 32-09-00-860-801 Put the Airplane in the Air Mode (P/B 201) 32-09-00-860-802 Return the Airplane to the Ground Mode (P/B 201) 36-00-00-860-805 Supply Pressure Upstream of the PRSOV (P/B 201) 36-00-00-860-806 Remove Pressure from the Pneumatic System (P/B 201) 78-31-00-010-801-F00 Open the Thrust Reverser (Selection) (P/B 201) 78-31-00-010-804-F00 Close the Thrust Reverser (Selection) (P/B 201)

C. Tools/Equipment

Reference Description STD-858 Tag - DO NOT OPERATE STD-1115 Source - Nitrogen, 0-100 PSIG STD-1198 Regulator - Pressure, 0-100 PSI with Pressure Gauge, 3/8 Inch ID Connections

D. Consumable Materials

E. Location Zones

Zone Area 510 Subzone - Left Wing: Leading Edge, Fwd of Front Spar, Inbd of Strut and Nacelle Gap Cover Area 520 Subzone - Left Wing: Leading Edge, Fwd of Front Spar, Outboard of Strut and Nacelle Gap Cover Area 610 Subzone - Right Wing: Leading Edge, Forward of Front Spar, Inboard of Nacelle Strut, Including Gap Cover Area 620 Subzone - Right Wing: Leading Edge, Forward of Front Spar, Outboard of Nacelle Strut 30-11-12 F. Prepare for the Test SUBTASK 30-11-12-860-004 (1) Make sure that each engine start lever is in the CUTOFF position. (a) Install DO NOT OPERATE tag, STD-858, on the each engine start lever. SUBTASK 30-11-12-010-003 WARNING DO THESE SPECIFIED TASKS IN THE CORRECT SEQUENCE BEFORE YOU OPEN THE THRUST REVERSER: RETRACT THE LEADING EDGE, DO THE DEACTIVATION OF THE LEADING EDGE AND THE THRUST REVERSER (FOR GROUND MAINTENANCE), AND OPEN THE FAN COWL PANELS. IF YOU DO NOT OBEY THE ABOVE SEQUENCE, INJURIES TO PERSONS AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR. (2) Do this task: Open the Thrust Reverser (Selection), TASK 78-31-00-010-801-F00. SUBTASK 30-11-12-860-005 (3) Make sure that the thrust levers are in the IDLE position.

G. Ground Wing TAI Solenoid Valve Test

SUBTASK 30-11-12-480-001 (1) Do these steps to connect the test equipment to the engine: (a) Disconnect the bleed air supply line [4] at the inlet tee at the supply pressure sense line [3] on the left of the engine. (b) Connect the 0-100 PSIG nitrogen source, STD-1115, pressure regulator, STD-1198, and test line at the tee to the supply pressure sense line [3]. SUBTASK 30-11-12-710-002 (2) Do this test of the ground wing TAI solenoid valve [1]: (a) Slowly increase the supply pressure to between 25 psig (1.76 kg/cm 2) and 30 psig (2.11 kg/cm2). (b) Make sure that the precooler control valve [2] goes closed. (c) Put the WING ANTI-ICE switch on the P5-11 engine and wing anti-ice control panel in the ON position. (d) Make sure that these actions occur: 1) Make sure that the precooler control valve [2] goes fully open, or within 30 degrees from fully open. SUBTASK 30-11-12-720-002 (3) Do this check of the air mode transition: WARNING OBEY THE PROCEDURE THAT PUTS THE AIRPLANE IN THE AIR MODE. IF YOU DO THE PROCEDURE INCORRECTLY , INJURIES TO PERSONNEL, AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR. (a) Do this task: Prepare to Put the Airplane in the Air Mode, TASK 32-09-00-840-801. (b) Do this task: Put the Airplane in the Air Mode, TASK 32-09-00-860-801. 1) Make sure that the precooler control valve [2] goes closed. (c) Make sure that the WING ANTI-ICE switch goes to the OFF position. (d) Put the WING ANTI-ICE switch to the ON position. 30-11-12 1) Make sure that the precooler control valve [2] remains closed. (e) Put the WING ANTI-ICE switch in the OFF position. (f) Decrease the supply pressure to 0 psig. (g) Return the airplane to the ground mode, do this task: Return the Airplane to the Ground Mode, TASK 32-09-00-860-802. SUBTASK 30-11-12-080-003 (4) Remove the supply pressure (Ps) test equipment and reinstall the bleed air supply line [4] to the supply pressure sense line [3] inlet tee as follows: (a) Remove the 0-100 PSIG nitrogen source, STD-1115, pressure regulator, STD-1198, and test line from the supply pressure sense line [3]. (b) Connect the bleed air supply line [4] to the inlet tee of the supply pressure sense line [3] (View B, Figure 501). 1) Apply a light coat of compound, D00010, or Pure Nickel Special compound, D00006, on the threads of all fittings when connecting the sense lines. CAUTION USE TWO WRENCHES TO LOOSEN OR TIGHTEN THE FITTING. IF YOU TWIST THE TUBE OR FITTING, YOU CAN CAUSE DAMAGE TO THEM. (c) Tighten the coupling nuts for the bleed air supply line [4] (Ps) to 133.0 in-lb (15.0 N·m) - 147.0 in-lb (17 N·m). CAUTION USE TWO WRENCHES TO LOOSEN OR TIGHTEN THE FITTING. IF YOU TWIST THE TUBE OR FITTING, YOU CAN CAUSE DAMAGE TO THEM. (d) Back off the coupling nuts to decrease the torque. CAUTION USE TWO WRENCHES TO LOOSEN OR TIGHTEN THE FITTING. IF YOU TWIST THE TUBE OR FITTING, YOU CAN CAUSE DAMAGE TO THEM. (e) Tighten the coupling nuts for the bleed air supply line [4] (Ps) to 133.0 in-lb (15.0 N·m) - 147.0 in-lb (17 N·m). SUBTASK 30-11-12-780-001 (5) Do this task: Supply Pressure Upstream of the PRSOV, TASK 36-00-00-860-805. SUBTASK 30-11-12-200-001 (6) Inspect and check for leaks at the bleed air supply line [4] at the inlet tee at the supply pressure sense line [3] on the left of the engine (Figure 501). NOTE: No leakage is permitted at the sense line connections. SUBTASK 30-11-12-780-002 (7) Do this task: Remove Pressure from the Pneumatic System, TASK 36-00-00-860-806. 30-11-12

H. Put the Airplane Back to Its Usual Condition.

SUBTASK 30-11-12-410-005 WARNING OBEY THE INSTRUCTIONS IN THE PROCEDURE TO CLOSE THE THRUST REVERSERS. IF YOU DO NOT OBEY THE INSTRUCTIONS WHEN YOU CLOSE THE THRUST REVERSERS, INJURIES TO PERSONS OR DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR. (1) Do this task: Close the Thrust Reverser (Selection), TASK 78-31-00-010-804-F00. SUBTASK 30-11-12-440-002 (2) Remove the DO-NOT-OPERATE tag from each engine start lever.

H. Wing Anti-Ice Telescoping Duct Seal Installation

SUBTASK 30-11-31-860-003 WARNING MAKE SURE THAT PERSONS AND EQUIPMENT ARE CLEAR OF THE LEADING EDGE SLATS. THE LEADING EDGE SLATS CAN MOVE QUICKLY . THIS CAN CAUSE INJURY TO PERSONS AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT . (1) If the actuator locks are not installed, do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Installation, TASK 27-81-00-480-801. SUBTASK 30-11-31-420-001 (2) Assemble the telescoping duct seal. (a) Put grease, D00013, on the inside diameter of the outer duct assembly. (b) Install the retainer [9], back up rings [2], seal [8], ring [7], and cap nut [4] on the inner telescoping duct [1] (Figure 401). 1) It is necessary to install seal [8] with the larger cutout facing to the outer telescoping duct [26] and the small dimple facing to cap nut [4] (View A-A, Figure 401). (c) Tighten the cap nut [4] to 250.0 in-lb (28.2 N·m) - 370.0 in-lb (41.8 N·m). (d) Install the lockwire [3] on the cap nut [4] (TASK 20-10-44-400-801). SUBTASK 30-11-31-420-012 (3) Install the new O-ring [11], new O-ring [12], or new O-ring [13], as necessary, on the swivel joint of the inner telescoping duct [1]. (a) If it is necessary use silicone compound, D00254, on the O-rings to make installation easier. SUBTASK 30-11-31-420-013 (4) To install the inner telescoping duct [1], do these steps: (a) Put the bearing [10] and bearing [14] on the ends of the inner telescoping duct [1]. (b) Put the inner telescoping duct [1] into position. (c) Install the bolts [16] and washers [15]. 1) Do not tighten the bolts [16] until after the supply duct [6] is installed. SUBTASK 30-11-31-420-014 (5) To install the supply duct [6], do these steps: (a) Clean the mating surfaces of the supply duct [6] tab and support assembly (SWPM 20-20-00). 1) Use cotton wiper, G00034, made moist with alcohol, B00130. 2) Immediately dry the surfaces with a new cotton wiper, G00034. (b) Apply a bead of sealant, A50231, along the top edge of the support assembly and bearings. (c) Put the supply duct [6] into position. (d) Install the bolts [17], bolts [18], and washers [15]. (e) Tighten the bolts [16], bolts [17], and bolts [18]. (f) Measure the electrical bonding resistance between the supply duct [6] tab and support assembly (SWPM 20-20-00). 1) Use an intrinsically safe approved bonding meter, COM-1550. 30-11-31 2) Make sure that the bonding resistance is 0.00070 ohms (0.70 milliohms) or less. (g) Fillet seal the supply duct [6] tab with sealant, A50231. (h) Position the clamps [5] so that there is a clearance of at least 0.2 in. (5.1 mm) from any adjacent structure. 1) Tighten the clamps [5] (TASK 20-50-11-910-801). a) Make sure that the loop bands do not touch. b) Make sure that the clearance between the retainers is 0.050 in. (1.270 mm) - 0.410 in. (10.414 mm).

SUBTASK 30-11-31-710-001 (1) Do this task: Wing Anti-Icing Duct - Leak Test, TASK 30-11-00-790-801.

J. Put the Airplane Back to Its Usual Condition

SUBTASK 30-11-31-410-012 (1) Install these panels, do this task: Leading Edge Access Panel Installation, TASK 57-41-02-400-801. (a) For the left wing, close these access panels: Number Name/Location 521DB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel 521KB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel 521RB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel (b) For the right wing, close these access panels: Number Name/Location 621DB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel 621JB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel 621QB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel SUBTASK 30-11-31-440-001 (2) Do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Removal, TASK 27-81-00-080-801.

E. Prepare for the Removal

SUBTASK 30-11-31-860-004 WARNING MAKE SURE THAT YOU INSTALL THE LEADING EDGE FLAP AND SLAT ACTUATORS LOCKOUT SET TO PREVENT ACCIDENTAL OPERATION. THE LEADING EDGE FLAP AND SLAT CAN MOVE QUICKLY . THIS CAN CAUSE INJURY TO PERSONS AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT . (1) Do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Installation, TASK 27-81-00-480-801. SUBTASK 30-11-31-010-013 (2) To get access to the telescoping duct, remove these panels (TASK 57-41-02-000-801). (a) For the left wing, open these access panels: Number Name/Location 521DB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel 521KB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel 521RB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel (b) For the right wing, open these access panels: Number Name/Location 621DB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel 621JB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel 621QB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel SUBTASK 30-11-31-020-012 (3) Remove the four bolts [38] that hold the bracket [39] to the outer telescoping duct [26]. NOTE: The following procedure is applicable to slats 2 through 7, unless otherwise stated. 30-11-31 SUBTASK 30-11-31-200-001 WARNING DO NOT LET OBJECTS GET IN THE HOUSING ASSEMBLY OF THE SLAT TRACK. THIS WILL HELP PREVENT A PUNCTURE OF THE HOUSING ASSEMBLY THAT COULD CAUSE A FUEL LEAK. THE FUEL LEAK COULD CAUSE A FIRE AND POSSIBLE DEATH OR INJURY TO PERSONNEL. (4) Keep clean and free of all Foreign Object Debris (FOD), the housing (can) assemblies of the slat main tracks, at all time. F. Outer Telescoping Duct Removal SUBTASK 30-11-31-010-003 (1) Do these steps to remove the inner skin panel from the outer slat. (a) Remove the backup plates [28]. NOTE: The backup plates hold the assembly seal. (b) Remove the seal [20]. (c) Remove the applicable cover panel [21]: 1) Open these access panels: Number Name/Location 522CB Slat No. 4 - Cove Skin 523CB Slat No. 3 - Cove Skin 524CB Slat No. 2 - Cove Skin 622CB Slat No. 5 - Cove Skin 623CB Slat No. 6 - Cove Skin 624CB Slat No. 7 - Cove Skin SUBTASK 30-11-31-010-008 (2) Do this step to remove the access door from the outer slat. (a) Remove the cover plate [22]. SUBTASK 30-11-31-020-010 (3) Do these steps to remove the blade seal [24]: (a) Remove the retaining bolts [32]. (b) Remove the washers [33], sleeves [34], springs [35], spacers [36], and spring retainers [37] (2 locations). (c) Remove the washer [33], washers [44], sleeve [34], spring [35], spacer [36], and spring retainer [37]. SUBTASK 30-11-31-030-002 (4) Remove the retaining bolts [25] and washers [43] from the spray duct [27] on both ends of the slat. SUBTASK 30-11-31-030-003 (5) Move the spray duct [27] out from the rib ends [31] until the outer telescoping duct [26], with the spray duct bearings [19], can be removed. (a) Remove the outer telescoping duct [26]. NOTE: If there is not sufficient clearance between angle details of the leading edge to remove the duct, do a modification of the leading edge angles (PAGEBLOCK 27-81-00/201). 30-11-31 SUBTASK 30-11-31-030-005 (6) Remove the spray duct bearings [19].

G. Inner Telescoping Duct Removal

SUBTASK 30-11-31-020-016 WARNING DO NOT LET OBJECTS GET IN THE HOUSING ASSEMBLY OF THE SLAT TRACK. THIS WILL HELP PREVENT A PUNCTURE OF THE HOUSING ASSEMBLY THAT COULD CAUSE A FUEL LEAK. THE FUEL LEAK COULD CAUSE A FIRE AND POSSIBLE DEATH OR INJURY TO PERSONNEL. (1) Do these steps to remove the supply ducts [6], bearing [10], and bearing [14]: (a) Remove the bolt [16], bolt [17], bolt [18], and washers [15]. (b) Disconnect the clamp [5] from one side of the supply duct [6]. 1) Remove the section from the supply ducts [6]. (c) Remove the bearing [10] and bearing [14]. SUBTASK 30-11-31-020-017 (2) Turn the inner telescoping duct [1] 90 degrees and lower the duct from the wing. SUBTASK 30-11-31-020-018 (3) Remove the O-ring [11], O-rings [12], and O-ring [13] from the swivel joint of the inner telescoping duct [1]. SUBTASK 30-11-31-020-019 (4) Discard the O-ring [11], O-rings [12] and O-ring [13].

H. Inner Telescoping Duct Installation

SUBTASK 30-11-31-420-015 (1) Install the O-ring [11], O-rings [12], and O-ring [13], as necessary, on the swivel joint of the inner telescoping duct [1]. (a) If it is necessary, use silicone compound, D00254, on the O-rings to make installation easier. SUBTASK 30-11-31-420-016 (2) To install the inner telescoping duct [1], do these steps: (a) Put the bearing [10] and bearing [14] on the ends of the inner telescoping duct [1]. (b) Put the inner telescoping duct [1] into position. (c) Install the bolts [16] and washers [15]. 1) Do not tighten the bolts until after the supply duct is installed. SUBTASK 30-11-31-420-017 (3) To install the supply duct [6], do these steps: (a) Clean the mating surfaces of the supply duct [6] tab and support assembly (SWPM 20-20-00). 1) Use a cotton wiper, G00034, made moist with alcohol, B00130. 2) Immediately dry the surfaces with a new cotton wiper, G00034. (b) Apply a bead of sealant, A50231, along the top edge of the support assembly and bearings. (c) Put the supply duct [6] into position. (d) Install the bolts [17], bolts [18], and washers [15]. (e) Tighten the bolts [16], bolts [17], and bolts [18]. (f) Measure the electrical bonding resistance between the supply duct [6] tab and the support assembly (SWPM 20-20-00). 1) Use an intrinsically safe approved bonding meter, COM-1550. 2) Make sure that the bonding resistance is 0.00070 ohms (0.70 milliohms) or less. (g) Fillet seal the supply duct [6] tab with sealant, A50231. (h) Position the clamps [5] so that there is a clearance of at least 0.2 in. (5.1 mm) from any adjacent structure. 1) Tighten the clamps [5] (TASK 20-50-11-910-801). a) Make sure that the loop bands do not touch. b) Make sure that the clearance between the retainers is 0.050 in. (1.270 mm) - 0.410 in. (10.414 mm).

I. Outer Telescoping Duct Installation

SUBTASK 30-11-31-430-006 (1) Put the spray duct bearings [19] on each end of the outer telescoping duct [26]. SUBTASK 30-11-31-430-007 (2) Put the outer telescoping duct [26] into the support brackets [30]. SUBTASK 30-11-31-430-008 (3) Do this to install spray duct [27]. (a) Clean the mating surfaces of the spray duct [27] flange, washers [43], and rib end [31]. 30-11-31 1) Use cotton wiper, G00034, made moist with alcohol, B00130. 2) Immediately dry the surfaces with a new cotton wiper, G00034. (b) Install the retaining bolts [25], washers [43], and spray duct [27]. SUBTASK 30-11-31-430-009 (4) Measure the electrical bonding resistance between the spray duct [27] flange and the rib end [31] (SWPM 20-20-00). (a) Use an intrinsically safe approved bonding meter, COM-1550. (b) Make sure that the bonding resistance is 0.00070 ohms (0.70 milliohms) or less. SUBTASK 30-11-31-420-010 (5) Fillet seal the spray duct [27] flange with Dow Corning 93-006-1 RF sealant, A00900, or sealant, A00160. SUBTASK 30-11-31-420-006 (6) Do these steps to install the blade seal [24]: (a) Connect the washer [33], washers [44], sleeve [34], spring [35], spacer [36], and spring retainer [37] to the blade seal [24]. (b) Connect the washers [33], sleeves [34], springs [35], spacers [36], and spring retainers [37] to the blade seal [24] (2 locations). (c) Install the retaining bolts [32] to attach the blade seal [24]. SUBTASK 30-11-31-410-002 (7) Do this step to install the access door: (a) Install the cover plate [22]. SUBTASK 30-11-31-410-009 (8) Do these steps to install the inner skin panel of the outer slat: (a) Install the applicable cover panel [21]: 1) Open these access panels: Number Name/Location 522CB Slat No. 4 - Cove Skin 523CB Slat No. 3 - Cove Skin 524CB Slat No. 2 - Cove Skin 622CB Slat No. 5 - Cove Skin 623CB Slat No. 6 - Cove Skin 624CB Slat No. 7 - Cove Skin (b) Install the seal [20]. (c) Install the backup plates [28]. 1) Apply compound, C00528, to the hex bolt on the lower backup plate [28].

J. Wing Anit-Icing Duct Leak Test

SUBTASK 30-11-31-790-002 (1) Do this task: Wing Anti-Icing Duct - Leak Test, TASK 30-11-00-790-801.

K. Put the Airplane Back to Its Usual Condition

SUBTASK 30-11-31-410-011 (1) Install these panels, do this task: Leading Edge Access Panel Installation, TASK 57-41-02-400-801. 30-11-31 (a) For the left wing, close these access panel: Number Name/Location 521DB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel 521KB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel 521RB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel (b) For the right wing, close these access panel: Number Name/Location 621DB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel 621JB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel 621QB Lower Leading Edge Access Panel SUBTASK 30-11-31-420-005 (2) Clean the mating surfaces of the ground strap [40] and bracket [39]. (a) Use cotton wiper, G00034, made moist with alcohol, B00130. (b) Immediately dry the surfaces with a new cotton wiper, G00034. SUBTASK 30-11-31-420-011 (3) Install the bolts [38], ground strap [40], washers [41], and nuts [42] that hold the bracket [39] to the outer telescoping duct [26]. (a) Adjust the vertical position of the bracket [39] to make sure that it is flush with the other access panels. (b) Tighten the bolts [38] after the vertical position of the bracket [39] is set. (c) Do a bonding check for the ground strap [40]. 1) Measure the electrical bonding resistance between door and duct (SWPM 20-20-00). a) Use and intrinsically safe approved bonding meter, COM-1550. b) Make sure that the bonding resistance is 0.010 ohms (10 milliohms) or less. (d) Apply sealant, A50231, to the terminal that was removed. SUBTASK 30-11-31-860-008 (4) Do this task: Leading Edge Flap and Slat Locks Removal, TASK 27-81-00-080-801. SUBTASK 30-11-31-410-004 (5) Adjust the telescoping duct door [2], if needed, to get equal clearances on the inboard and outboard sides, and equal clearances and on the forward and aft sides (Figure 402).

30-11-41-000-801Engine and Wing Anti-Ice Panel Removal ATA 30 · Task 000-801

Task Description

2. Engine and Wing Anti-Ice Panel Removal (Figure 401)

A. General

(1) This task gives instructions to remove the engine and wing anti-ice panel.

B. References

Reference Title 20-40-12-000-802 ESDS Handling for Metal Encased Unit Removal (P/B 201)

C. Location Zones

Zone Area 212 Flight Compartment - Right

D. Prepare for the Removal

SUBTASK 30-11-41-860-012 (1) Open these circuit breakers and install safety tags: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 1 C00146 ANTI-ICE & RAIN WING ANTI-ICE VALVE A 6 C00148 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 1 & WING CONT A 7 C01001 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 1 COWL AI VALVE B 6 C00149 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENGINE 2 CONTROL B 7 C01002 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 2 COWL AI VALVE SUBTASK 30-11-41-010-002 (2) Open the P5 overhead panel. Task Description

2. Engine and Wing Thermal Anti-Ice Panel (P5-11) Test (Figure 501)

A. General

(1) This test makes sure that the Engine and Wing Anti-Ice Panel (P5-11) is operating properly.

B. References

Reference Title 24-22-00-860-813 Supply External Power (P/B 201) 24-22-00-860-814 Remove External Power (P/B 201) 34-43-00-040-801 Weather Radar (WXR) System - Deactivation (P/B 201) 34-43-00-440-801 Weather Radar (WXR) System - Activation (P/B 201) 36-00-00-860-802 Supply Pressure to the Pneumatic System with an External Ground Air Source (P/B 201) 36-00-00-860-803 Supply Pressure to the Pneumatic System with the APU (P/B 201) 36-00-00-860-806 Remove Pressure from the Pneumatic System (P/B 201) 71-11-02-010-801-F00 Open the Fan Cowl Panels (P/B 201) 71-11-02-410-801-F00 Close the Fan Cowl Panels (P/B 201) 78-31-00-010-801-F00 Open the Thrust Reverser (Selection) (P/B 201)

D. Prepare for the Tests

SUBTASK 30-11-41-860-001 (1) Do this task: Supply External Power, TASK 24-22-00-860-813. SUBTASK 30-11-41-860-002 (2) Provide pneumatic pressure for the Thermal Anti-Ice (TAI) ducts. (a) To use the Auxiliary Power Unit (APU) to pressurize the TAI duct: 1) Do this task: Supply Pressure to the Pneumatic System with the APU, TASK 36-00-00-860-803. (b) To use an external air supply to pressurize the TAI duct: 1) Do this task: Supply Pressure to the Pneumatic System with an External Ground Air Source, TASK 36-00-00-860-802. (c) Put the BLEED 1 and 2 switches on the air conditioning panel in the OFF position. (d) Put the L PACK AND R PACK switches on the air conditioning panel in the OFF position. (e) Make sure that the ISOLATION VALVE switch is in the OPEN or the AUTO position. SUBTASK 30-11-41-010-001 (3) For the applicable engine, open the right fan cowl panel (TASK 71-11-02-010-801-F00). 30-11-41 SUBTASK 30-11-41-860-003 (4) Make sure that the thrust levers are in the IDLE position. SUBTASK 30-11-41-040-001 (5) Do this task: Weather Radar (WXR) System - Deactivation, TASK 34-43-00-040-801.

E. Test the WING ANTI-ICE Switch and Indication

SUBTASK 30-11-41-860-004 (1) Press and release the L VALVE OPEN and R VALVE OPEN light switches on the P5-11 panel. (a) Make sure that each light comes on then goes off. SUBTASK 30-11-41-860-005 CAUTION DO NOT OPERATE THE WING ANTI-ICE SYSTEM MORE THAN 30 SECONDS. DAMAGE TO THE AIRCRAFT CAN OCCUR. (2) Make sure that the engine and wing anti-ice panel commands the anti-icing valves open: (a) Put the WING ANTI-ICE switch in the ON position. (b) Make sure that the VALVE OPEN lights come on brightly for 1 to 6 seconds. (c) Make sure that the VALVE OPEN lights are on dimly after 6 seconds. SUBTASK 30-11-41-720-001 (3) Check the thrust switch inputs to the engine and wing anti-ice panel: (a) Put the thrust levers in the full throttle position. 1) Make sure that the L and R VALVE OPEN lights come on brightly. (b) Put the thrust levers in the IDLE position. (c) Make sure that the VALVE OPEN lights are on dimly after 6 seconds. SUBTASK 30-11-41-860-006 (4) Make sure that the engine and wing anti-ice panel commands the anti-icing valves closed: (a) Put the WING ANTI-ICE switch in the OFF position. (b) Make sure that the VALVE OPEN lights come on brightly for 1 to 6 seconds. (c) Make sure that the VALVE OPEN lights go off after 6 seconds. F. Test the ENG ANTI-ICE Switches and Indication SUBTASK 30-11-41-860-007 (1) Press and release the COWL ANTI-ICE and COWL VALVE OPEN light switches on the P5-11 panel. (a) Make sure that each light comes on then goes off. SUBTASK 30-11-41-860-015 (2) Manually open the Pressure Regulating and Shutoff Valve (PRSOV) valve on each engine: 30-11-41 WARNING DO THESE SPECIFIED TASKS IN THE CORRECT SEQUENCE BEFORE YOU OPEN THE THRUST REVERSERS: RETRACT THE LEADING EDGE, DO THE DEACTIVATION PROCEDURES FOR THE LEADING EDGE AND THE THRUST REVERSER (FOR GROUND MAINTENANCE), AND OPEN THE FAN COWL PANELS. IF YOU DO NOT OBEY THE ABOVE SEQUENCE, INJURIES TO PERSONS AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR. (a) Open the left thrust reverser (TASK 78-31-00-010-801-F00). (b) Put the applicable engine BLEED switch on the P5-10, forward overhead panel to the ON position. (c) Use a wrench on the manual override nut for the PRSOV to put it to the open position. (d) Make sure that the PRSOV stays in the open position. NOTE: The PRSOV is spring-loaded to the closed position. If there is pressure supplied and the PRSOV does not stay open, there may be a leakage in the PRSOV or in the sense line(s). SUBTASK 30-11-41-860-008 (3) Make sure that the engine and wing anti-ice panel commands the cowl anti-icing valves open: (a) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 1 switch in the ON position. 1) Make sure that the left COWL VALVE OPEN light comes on brightly for 1 to 6 seconds. 2) Make sure that the COWL VALVE OPEN light goes dimly after 6 seconds. (b) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 2 switch in the ON position. 1) Make sure that the right COWL VALVE OPEN light comes on brightly for 1 to 6 seconds. 2) Make sure that the COWL VALVE OPEN light goes dimly after 6 seconds. SUBTASK 30-11-41-720-002 (4) Check the cowl TAI duct pressure switch inputs to the engine and wing anti-ice panel: (a) Open these circuit breakers and install safety tags: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 6 C00148 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 1 & WING CONT A 7 C01001 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 1 COWL AI VALVE (b) Remove the electrical connector from the left cowl TAI duct pressure switch. (c) Connect a jumper wire between pins 1 and 2 of the electrical connector. 1) Make sure that the left COWL ANTI-ICE light comes on. (d) Remove the jumper wire. (e) Connect the electrical connector to the pressure switch. 1) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 1 switch in the OFF position. 2) Make sure that the left COWL ANTI-ICE light goes off. 30-11-41 (f) Remove the safety tags and close these circuit breakers: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name A 6 C00148 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 1 & WING CONT A 7 C01001 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 1 COWL AI VALVE (g) Open these circuit breakers and install safety tags: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name B 6 C00149 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENGINE 2 CONTROL B 7 C01002 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 2 COWL AI VALVE (h) Remove the electrical connector from the right cowl TAI duct pressure switch. (i) Connect a jumper wire between pins 1 and 2 of the electrical connector. 1) Make sure that the right COWL ANTI-ICE light comes on. (j) Remove the jumper wire. (k) Connect the electrical connector to the pressure switch. 1) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 2 switch in the OFF position. 2) Make sure that the right COWL ANTI-ICE light goes off. (l) Remove the safety tags and close these circuit breakers: CAPT Electrical System Panel, P18-3 Row Col Number Name B 6 C00149 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENGINE 2 CONTROL B 7 C01002 ANTI-ICE & RAIN ENG 2 COWL AI VALVE SUBTASK 30-11-41-860-009 (5) Make sure that the engine and wing anti-ice panel commands the cowl anti-icing valves closed: (a) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 1 switch in the OFF position. 1) Make sure that the left COWL VALVE OPEN light comes on brightly for 1 to 3 seconds. 2) Make sure that the COWL VALVE OPEN light goes off after 3 seconds. (b) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 2 switch in the OFF position. 1) Make sure that the right COWL VALVE OPEN light comes on brightly for 1 to 3 seconds. 2) Make sure that the COWL VALVE OPEN light goes off after 3 seconds.

G. Put the airplane back to its usual condition.

SUBTASK 30-21-00-910-001 CAUTION DO NOT KEEP THE COWL TAI VALVE OPEN FOR MORE THAN THE SPECIFIED TIME WHEN THE TEMPERATURE IS MORE THAN THE SPECIFIED LIMIT . DAMAGE TO THE INLET COWL CAN OCCUR. (1) If the outside air temperature is more than 50°F (10°C), operate the engine cowl anti-icing system for 30 seconds or less. NOTE: The temperature limit of 50°F (10°C) is only applicable to the engine cowl anti-ice valve installation/system test. 30-21-00 SUBTASK 30-21-00-910-002 (2) There is no time limit for operation of the engine cowl anti-icing system when the temperature is at or less than 50°F (10°C). SUBTASK 30-21-00-840-002 (3) Do one of the following steps to provide pneumatic pressure for the ducts: (a) Use the engines to pressurize the TAI duct, do this task: Start the Engine Procedure (Selection), TASK 71-00-00-800-807-F00. (b) Use the Auxiliary Power Unit (APU) to pressurize the TAI duct, do this task: Supply Pressure to the Pneumatic System with the APU, TASK 36-00-00-860-803. (c) Use an external air supply to pressurize the TAI duct, do this task: Supply Pressure to the Pneumatic System with an External Ground Air Source, TASK 36-00-00-860-802. (d) If you use the APU, external air, or a single engine to pressurize the TAI duct, then do these steps: 1) Put the BLEED 1 and 2 switches on the air conditioning panel in the OFF position. 2) Put the L PACK and R PACK switches on the air conditioning panel in the OFF position. 3) Make sure that the ISOLATION VALVE switch is in the OPEN or AUTO position. 4) If you use the APU or external air to pressurize the TAI duct, then you must manually open thePressure Regulating and Shutoff Valve (PRSOV) valves. 5) If you use the a single engine to pressurize the TAI duct, then you must manually open the PRSOV valve on the non-running engine. 6) Open a PRSOV valve, do these steps on the applicable engine or engines: WARNING DO THESE SPECIFIED TASKS IN THE CORRECT SEQUENCE BEFORE YOU OPEN THE THRUST REVERSERS: RETRACT THE LEADING EDGE, DO THE DEACTIVATION PROCEDURES FOR THE LEADING EDGE AND THE THRUST REVERSER (FOR GROUND MAINTENANCE), AND OPEN THE FAN COWL PANELS. IF YOU DO NOT OBEY THE ABOVE SEQUENCE, INJURIES TO PERSONS AND DAMAGE TO EQUIPMENT CAN OCCUR. a) Open the left thrust reverser, do this task: Open the Thrust Reverser (Selection), TASK 78-31-00-010-801-F00. b) Put the applicable engine BLEED switch, on the P5-10 forward overhead panel, to the ON position. c) Use a wrench on the manual override nut for the PRSOV to put it to the OPEN position. d) Make sure that the PRSOV stays in the OPEN position. NOTE: The PRSOV is spring-loaded to the closed position. If there is pressure supplied and the PRSOV does not stay open, there may be a leakage in the PRSOV or in the sense line(s). SUBTASK 30-21-00-860-003 (4) Do this test of the COWL VALVE OPEN and COWL ANTI-ICE light switches: (a) Push and release the COWL VALVE OPEN and COWL ANTI-ICE light switches on the P5-11 panel. 30-21-00 (b) Make sure that each light comes on then goes off. SUBTASK 30-21-00-860-004 (5) Make sure that the engine and wing anti-ice panel commands the cowl TAI valves open. NOTE: If the COWL VALVE OPEN lights remain illuminated bright with engines at IDLE, position APU BLEED air switch to OFF and increase thrust slightly (up to a maximum of 30% N1). (a) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 1 switch in the ON position. 1) Make sure that the left COWL VALVE OPEN light comes on brightly within 6 seconds. 2) Make sure that the COWL VALVE OPEN light becomes dim after it comes on brightly. 3) Make sure that the green TAI indication shows on the Common Display System (CDS) display above the left N1 speed display. (b) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 2 switch in the ON position. 1) Make sure that the right COWL VALVE OPEN light comes on brightly within 6 seconds. 2) Make sure that the COWL VALVE OPEN light becomes dim after it comes on brightly. 3) Make sure that the green TAI indication shows on the CDS display above the right N1 speed display. SUBTASK 30-21-00-860-005 (6) Make sure that the engine and wing anti-ice panel commands the cowl TAI valves closed. (a) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 1 switch in the OFF position. 1) Make sure that the left COWL VALVE OPEN light comes on brightly within 3 seconds. 2) Make sure that the COWL VALVE OPEN light then goes off. (b) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 2 switch in the OFF position. 1) Make sure that the right COWL VALVE OPEN light comes on brightly within 3 seconds. 2) Make sure that the COWL VALVE OPEN light then goes off. SUBTASK 30-21-00-860-010 (7) Allow the PRSOV to return to the CLOSED position by spring force only. NOTE: It is allowable to use slight hand pressure on the wrench at the end of the stroke to assist the valve to the full closed position. The valve is designed to have air pressure to assist the valve closure to overcome the resistance of the valve's butterfly seal. (a) If the PRSOV does not move smooth or return to the CLOSED position on the third cycle, replace the valve, do these tasks: • PRSOV Removal, TASK 36-11-04-000-801, • PRSOV Installation, TASK 36-11-04-400-801. SUBTASK 30-21-00-860-006 (8) If you used the engines to pressurize the TAI ducts, then stop the engines. 30-21-00 (a) Do this task: Stop the Engine Procedure (Usual Engine Stop), TASK 71-00-00-700-819-F00. SUBTASK 30-21-00-860-009 (9) If you used the APU or external air to pressurize the TAI ducts, do this task: Remove Pressure from the Pneumatic System, TASK 36-00-00-860-806. SUBTASK 30-21-00-860-007 (10) Make sure that the fuel shutoff levers are in the cutoff position and install the DO NOT OPERATE tags, STD-858. SUBTASK 30-21-00-010-001 (11) For the right fan cowl panel, do this task: Open the Fan Cowl Panels, TASK 71-11-02-010-801-F00. SUBTASK 30-21-00-720-004 (12) Do this test of the left cowl TAI duct overpressure detection circuit: (a) Disconnect the electrical connector from the left engine duct overpressure switch. (b) Connect a jumper wire between pins 1 and 2 of the connector. 1) Make sure that the left COWL ANTI-ICE light comes on. (c) Remove the jumper wire from the connector. (d) Re-connect the connector to the overpressure switch. (e) Make sure that the left COWL ANTI-ICE light is off. SUBTASK 30-21-00-720-005 (13) Do this test of the right cowl TAI duct overpressure detection circuit: (a) Disconnect the electrical connector from the right engine duct overpressure switch. (b) Connect a jumper wire between pins 1 and 2 of the connector. 1) Make sure that the right COWL ANTI-ICE light comes on. (c) Remove the jumper wire from the connector. (d) Connect the connector to the overpressure switch. (e) Make sure that the right COWL ANTI-ICE light is off. SUBTASK 30-21-00-720-003 (14) Do this test to make sure that the TAI fault is shown: (a) Do a test of the left cowl TAI system. 1) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 1 switch in the ON position. 2) Make sure that the amber TAI indication is shown on the CDS display above the left N1 speed display. 3) Make sure that the left COWL VALVE OPEN light comes on brightly. 4) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 1 switch in the OFF position. 5) Make sure that the TAI indication on the CDS display goes away. 6) Make sure that the COWL VALVE OPEN light goes off. (b) Do a test of the right cowl TAI system. 1) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 2 switch in the ON position. 2) Make sure that the amber TAI indication is shown on the CDS display above the right N1 speed display. 30-21-00 3) Make sure that the right COWL VALVE OPEN light comes on brightly. 4) Put the ENG ANTI-ICE 2 switch in the OFF position. 5) Make sure that the TAI indication on the CDS display goes away. 6) Make sure that the COWL VALVE OPEN light goes off. F. Put the Airplane Back to Its Usual Condition SUBTASK 30-21-00-410-002 WARNING OBEY THE INSTRUCTIONS IN THE PROCEDURE TO CLOSE THE THRUST REVERSERS. SUBTASK 30-21-00-410-001 (2) Close the right fan cowl panel, do this task: Close the Fan Cowl Panels, TASK 71-11-02-410-801-F00. SUBTASK 30-21-11-020-001 (1) Disconnect the electrical connector DP1303 [10] from the valve [5]. SUBTASK 30-21-11-020-002 (2) Remove the bolt [6], washer [7], and nut [9] that attach the bonding jumper [8] to the valve [5]. (a) Move the bonding jumper [8] away from the valve [5]. SUBTASK 30-21-11-020-007 (3) If it is necessary to remove the bonding jumper [8] from the flange, do these steps: CAUTION MAKE SURE THAT YOU ONLY USE APPROVED SCRAPERS ON THE AIRPLANE SKIN. SCRAPERS THAT ARE NOT APPROVED CAN MAKE SCRATCHES ON THE SKIN AND CAUSE FATIGUE CRACKS. (a) Remove all of the sealant from the connection of the bonding jumper [8] and flange. 1) Use a sealant removal tool, COM-2481, and sealant removal tool handle, COM-4746. 2) If it is necessary, do these steps to sharpen the scraper. 30-21-11 a) Sharpen the scraper with sandpaper, G50260, or finer by hand or by metal sharpening wheel (scraper sharpener, COM-4747). b) If the scraper is sharpened by hand, rub the sharpened scraper on a wiper, G50262, to remove loose sanding particles. c) If a metal sharpening wheel is used, rub the sharpened scraper on sandpaper, G50261, or finer aluminum oxide paper to remove debris and then rub it on wiper, G50262, to remove loose sanding particles. (b) Remove the bolt [13], washers [14], and nut [15], that attach the bonding jumper [8] to the flange. (c) Remove the bonding jumper [8]. SUBTASK 30-21-11-020-003 (4) Remove the bolts [11] and nuts [12] that attach the aft duct to the fan case bracket. SUBTASK 30-21-11-020-004 (5) Do these steps to remove the valve [5]: (a) Disconnect the couplings [1] at each end of the valve [5]. CAUTION DO NOT APPLY TOO MUCH FORCE TO THE AFT DUCT . DAMAGE TO THE AFT DUCT CAN OCCUR. (b) Push the aft duct rearward to disengage the duct flanges. NOTE: T emporary support can be installed between the fan case bracket and the aft duct to hold the duct in this position. This will make the removal of the valve easier. (c) Remove the valve [5] from the engine. (d) Remove the seal [2] from the forward end of the valve [5] and aft duct flange. (e) Install the protective covers on the openings of the ducts and valve [5].
